\begin{split}&\left|\mu^{D}p_{\mu,\sigma}({\bf x})-\sum_{j_{1},\ldots,j_{D}=1}^{m}\prod_{k=1}^{D}\left\{\widetilde{v}_{j_{k}}\phi\left(\widetilde{y}_{j_{k}}^{(k)}\right)\right\}F_{(j_{1},\ldots,j_{D})}\right|\\
&\leq\epsilon\left(1+\sum_{i=2}^{D}\sum_{j_{1},\ldots,j_{i-1}=1}^{m}\frac{|\prod_{k=1}^{i-1}\widetilde{v}_{j_{k}}|}{(2\pi)^{\frac{i-1}{2}}}\right)\\
&\leq D_{3}K\left(\sigma^{\tau_{\rm tail}}+m^{-\beta}\{\log(1/\sigma)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(\beta+1)}\right)\{\log(1/\sigma)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(D-1)},\end{split}

(42)

where
D_{3}=DD_{2}(D_{1}/\sqrt{2\pi})^{D-1}
.

Note that

\displaystyle\nabla p_{\mu,\sigma}({\bf x})=\int_{\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1}\left(\frac{\mu{\bf y}-{\bf x}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)p_{0}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma}({\bf x}-\mu{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}

\displaystyle=\sigma^{-1}\mu^{-D}\int_{\|{\bf x}+\sigma{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu}(y_{1},\ldots,y_{D})^{\top}p_{0}\left(\frac{{\bf x}+\sigma{\bf y}}{\mu}\right)\prod_{i=1}^{D}\phi(y_{i}){\rm d}{\bf y}.

For
i\in[D]
, consider a one-dimensional real-valued function
\widetilde{g}_{i}
such that

\displaystyle\widetilde{g}_{i}\left(y;{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i}\right)=yg_{i}\left(y;{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i}\right),\quad y\in\left[(-x_{i}-\mu)/\sigma,(-x_{i}+\mu)/\sigma\right].

Then,

\begin{split}&\left|\int_{\frac{-x_{i}-\mu}{\sigma}}^{\frac{-x_{i}+\mu}{\sigma}}\widetilde{g}_{i}\left(y;{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i}\right){\rm d}y-\int_{(-x_{i}-\mu)D_{\sigma}}^{(-x_{i}+\mu)D_{\sigma}}\widetilde{g}_{i}\left(y;{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i}\right){\rm d}y\right|\\
&\leq\left|\int_{\frac{-x_{i}-\mu}{\sigma}}^{(-x_{i}-\mu)D_{\sigma}}\widetilde{g}_{i}\left(y;{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i}\right){\rm d}y\right|+\left|\int^{\frac{-x_{i}+\mu}{\sigma}}_{(-x_{i}+\mu)D_{\sigma}}\widetilde{g}_{i}\left(y;{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i}\right){\rm d}y\right|\\
&\leq K\int^{\infty}_{(x_{i}+\mu)D_{\sigma}}y\phi(y){\rm d}y+K\int_{(-x_{i}+\mu)D_{\sigma}}^{\infty}y\phi(y){\rm d}y\\
&=K\exp\left(-\frac{(x_{i}+\mu)^{2}D_{\sigma}^{2}}{2}\right)+K\exp\left(-\frac{(-x_{i}+\mu)^{2}D_{\sigma}^{2}}{2}\right)\\
&\leq 2K\exp\left(-\tau_{\rm tail}\log(1/\sigma)\right)=2K\sigma^{\tau_{\rm tail}},\end{split}

(43)

where the first equality holds because

\displaystyle-x_{i}-\mu\leq-\{\log(1/\sigma)\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}/2<0\text{\quad and \quad}-x_{i}+\mu\geq 2^{-1}\{\log(1/\sigma)\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}>0.

Since
\phi^{\prime}(y)=-y\phi(y)
for
y\in{\mathbb{R}}
and
\sigma/\mu<1
, Lemma
21
implies that
\widetilde{g}_{i}\in\mathcal{H}_{1}^{\beta,KC_{G,1}}((-x_{i}-\mu)D_{\sigma},(-x_{i}+\mu)D_{\sigma}])
.
Moreover, Lemma
20
implies that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{(-x_{i}-\mu)D_{\sigma}}^{(-x_{i}+\mu)D_{\sigma}}\widetilde{g}_{i}\left(y;{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i}\right){\rm d}y-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\widetilde{v}_{j}\widetilde{y}_{j}^{(i)}g_{i}\left(\widetilde{y}_{j}^{(i)};{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\frac{2^{\lfloor\beta\rfloor+1}{{n_{\beta}}}^{\beta}}{\lfloor\beta\rfloor!}\right\}C_{G,1}K\mu^{\beta+1}D_{\sigma}^{\beta+1}m^{-\beta}

because
\widetilde{g}_{i}(y;{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i})=yg_{i}(y;{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i})
.
Combining (
43
) with the last display, we have

\left|\int_{\frac{-x_{i}-\mu}{\sigma}}^{\frac{-x_{i}+\mu}{\sigma}}p_{0}\left(\frac{{\bf x}+\sigma({\bf y}_{<i},y,{\bf y}_{>i})}{\mu}\right)y\phi(y){\rm d}y-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\widetilde{v}_{j}\widetilde{y}_{j}^{(i)}g_{i}\left(\widetilde{y}_{j}^{(i)};{\bf y}_{<i},{\bf y}_{>i}\right)\right|\leq\epsilon.

(44)

Combining (
39
) with a simple calculation, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\|{\bf x}_{-i}+\sigma{\bf y}_{-i}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu}p_{0}\left(\frac{{\bf x}+\sigma({\bf y}_{<i},y,{\bf y}_{>i})}{\mu}\right)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq i\end{subarray}}^{D}\phi(y_{k}){\rm d}{\bf y}_{<i}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\left.\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1},\ldots,j_{D}=1\\
{\rm w/o}\ j_{i}\end{subarray}}^{m}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq i\end{subarray}}^{D}\left\{\widetilde{v}_{j_{k}}\phi\left(\widetilde{y}_{j_{k}}^{(k)}\right)\right\}p_{0}\left(\frac{{\bf x}+\sigma\left(\widetilde{y}_{j_{1}}^{(1)},\ldots,\widetilde{y}_{j_{i-1}}^{(i-1)},y,\widetilde{y}_{j_{i+1}}^{(i+1)},\ldots,\widetilde{y}_{j_{D}}^{(D)}\right)^{\top}}{\mu}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon\left(1+\sum_{h=1}^{D-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1},\ldots,j_{h}=1\\
{\rm w/o}\ j_{i}\end{subarray}}^{m}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq i\end{subarray}}^{h}\left\{|\widetilde{v}_{j_{k}}|\phi\left(\widetilde{y}_{j_{k}}^{(k)}\right)\right\}\right)

for any
y\in{\mathbb{R}}
satisfying
|x_{i}+\sigma y|\leq\mu
,
where
\sum_{j_{1},\ldots,j_{D}=1\ {\rm w/o}\ j_{i}}^{m}
denotes the summation over

\displaystyle 1\leq j_{1},\ldots,j_{i-1},j_{i+1},\ldots,j_{D}\leq m.

Note that

\displaystyle\mu^{D}\sigma(\nabla p_{\mu,\sigma}({\bf x}))_{i}=\int_{\|{\bf x}+\sigma{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu}y_{i}p_{0}\left(\frac{{\bf x}+\sigma{\bf y}}{\mu}\right)\prod_{k=1}^{D}\phi(y_{k}){\rm d}{\bf y}

\displaystyle=\int_{\frac{-x_{i}-\mu}{\sigma}}^{\frac{-x_{i}+\mu}{\sigma}}\left\{\int_{\|{\bf x}_{-i}+\sigma{\bf y}_{-i}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu}p_{0}\left(\frac{{\bf x}+\sigma{\bf y}}{\mu}\right)\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq i\end{subarray}}^{D}\phi(y_{k}){\rm d}{\bf y}_{<i}\right\}y_{i}\phi(y_{i}){\rm d}y_{i}.

Combining (
44
) with the last three displays, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\|{\bf x}+\sigma{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu}y_{i}p_{0}\left(\frac{{\bf x}+\sigma{\bf y}}{\mu}\right)\prod_{k=1}^{D}\phi(y_{k}){\rm d}{\bf y}-\sum_{j_{1},\ldots,j_{D}=1}^{m}\widetilde{y}_{j_{i}}^{(i)}\prod_{k=1}^{D}\left\{\widetilde{v}_{j_{k}}\phi\left(\widetilde{y}_{j_{k}}^{(k)}\right)\right\}F_{(j_{1},\ldots,j_{D})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon\left(1+\sum_{h=1}^{D-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1},\ldots,j_{h}=1\\
{\rm w/o}\ j_{i}\end{subarray}}^{m}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq i\end{subarray}}^{h}\left\{|\widetilde{v}_{j_{k}}|\phi\left(\widetilde{y}_{j_{k}}^{(k)}\right)\right\}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1},\ldots,j_{D}=1\\
{\rm w/o}\ j_{i}\end{subarray}}^{m}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq i\end{subarray}}^{D}\left\{|\widetilde{v}_{j_{k}}|\phi\left(\widetilde{y}_{j_{k}}^{(k)}\right)\right\}\right)\int_{\frac{-x_{i}-\mu}{\sigma}}^{\frac{-x_{i}+\mu}{\sigma}}|y|\phi(y){\rm d}y

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\epsilon}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(1+\sum_{h=1}^{D}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1},\ldots,j_{h}=1\\
{\rm w/o}\ j_{i}\end{subarray}}^{m}\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq i\end{subarray}}^{D}\frac{|\widetilde{v}_{j_{k}}|}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\right),

where the last inequality holds because
\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|y|\phi(y)=2\int_{0}^{\infty}y\phi(y){\rm d}y=\frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}}
and
|\phi|\leq 1/\sqrt{2\pi}
.
Combining with (
42
), the last display is bounded by

\displaystyle\frac{2D_{3}K}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(\sigma^{\tau_{\rm tail}}+m^{-\beta}\{\log(1/\sigma)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(\beta+1)}\right)\{\log(1/\sigma)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(D-1)},

and the assertion follows by re-defining constants.
∎

B.2
Proof of Proposition
23

Proof.

Let
\delta>0
be a small enough value as described below.
There exists neural networks
f_{\mu}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\mu},{\bf d}_{\mu},s_{\mu},M_{\mu}),f_{\sigma}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\sigma},{\bf d}_{\sigma},s_{\sigma},M_{\sigma})
with

\displaystyle L_{\mu},L_{\sigma}\leq C_{N,4}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{2},\quad\|{\bf d}_{\mu}\|_{\infty},\|{\bf d}_{\sigma}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{N,4}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{2}

\displaystyle s_{\mu},s_{\sigma}\leq C_{N,4}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{3},\quad M_{\mu},M_{\sigma}\leq C_{N,4}\log(1/\delta)

such that

\left|\mu_{t}-f_{\mu}(t)\right|\leq\delta\quad\quad\text{and}\quad\quad\left|\sigma_{t}-f_{\sigma}(t)\right|\leq\delta

(45)

for
t\geq\delta
, where
C_{N,4}
is the constant in Lemma
18
.
for any
0\leq t\leq(2\overline{\tau})^{-1}
.

Since
\log(1/x)=-\log x
for any
x>0
, Lemma
16
implies that there exists a positive constant
D_{1}=D_{1}(\underline{\tau})
and neural network
f_{\log}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\log},{\bf d}_{\log},s_{\log},M_{\log})
with

\displaystyle L_{\log}\leq D_{1}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{2}\log\log(1/\delta),\quad\|{\bf d}_{\log}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{1}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{3}

\displaystyle s_{\log}\leq D_{1}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{5}\log\log(1/\delta),\quad M_{\log}\leq\exp\left(D_{1}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{2}\right)

such that
\left|\log(1/x)-f_{\log}(\widetilde{x})\right|\leq\sqrt{\underline{\tau}\delta}/2+(2/\sqrt{\underline{\tau}\delta})|x-\widetilde{x}|

for
\sqrt{\underline{\tau}\delta}/2\leq x\leq(\sqrt{\underline{\tau}\delta}/2)^{-1}
and
\widetilde{x}\in{\mathbb{R}}
.
Combining with (
45
), we have

\begin{split}\left|\log(1/\sigma_{t})-f_{\rm log}(f_{\sigma}(t))\right|\leq\frac{2\sqrt{\delta}}{\sqrt{\underline{\tau}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\underline{\tau}\delta}}{2}=\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{\underline{\tau}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\underline{\tau}}}{2}\right)\sqrt{\delta}\end{split}

(46)

for
\delta\leq t\leq(2\overline{\tau})^{-1}
.
Let

\displaystyle\tau_{\rm mult}=\tau_{\rm bd}+1

Lemma
14
implies that for
k\geq 2
, there exists a neural network
f_{\rm mult}^{(k)}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\rm mult}^{(k)},{\bf d}_{\rm mult}^{(k)},s_{\rm mult}^{(k)},M_{\rm mult}^{(k)})
with

\displaystyle L_{\rm mult}^{(k)}\leq C_{N,1}(k\tau_{\rm mult}+1)\log k\log(1/\delta),\quad\|{\bf d}_{\rm mult}^{(k)}\|_{\infty}=48k,\quad

\displaystyle s_{\rm mult}^{(k)}\leq C_{N,1}k(\tau_{\rm mult}+1)\log(1/\delta),\quad M_{\rm mult}^{(k)}=\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{k\tau_{\rm mult}}

such that

\left|f_{\rm mult}^{(k)}(\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{k})-\prod_{i=1}^{k}x_{i}\right|\leq\delta+k\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{(k-1)\tau_{\rm mult}}\widetilde{\epsilon}

(47)

for any
{\bf x}=(x_{1},\ldots,x_{k})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k}
with
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{\tau_{\rm mult}}
and
\widetilde{\bf x}=(\widetilde{x}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{x}_{k})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k}
with
\|{\bf x}-\widetilde{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{\epsilon}
,
where
0<\widetilde{\epsilon}\leq 1
and
C_{N,1}
is the constant in Lemma
14
.
Combining Lemma
11
and Lemma
9
with the last display, there exists a neural network
f_{\rm pow}^{(k)}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\rm pow}^{(k)},{\bf d}_{\rm pow}^{(k)},s_{\rm pow}^{(k)},M_{\rm pow}^{(k)})
with

\displaystyle L_{\rm pow}^{(k)}=L_{\rm mult}^{(k)}+2,\quad\|{\bf d}_{\rm pow}^{(k)}\|_{\infty}\leq 96k,\quad s_{\rm pow}^{(k)}\leq 2s_{\rm mult}^{(k)}+8k,\quad M_{\rm pow}^{(k)}=M_{\rm mult}^{(k)}\vee 1

such that

\left|f_{\rm pow}^{(k)}(\widetilde{x})-x^{k}\right|\leq\delta+k\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{(k-1)\tau_{\rm mult}}\widetilde{\epsilon}

(48)

for any
|x|\leq\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{\tau_{\rm mult}}
and
\widetilde{x}\in{\mathbb{R}}
with
|x-\widetilde{x}|\leq\widetilde{\epsilon}
.
For
\delta\leq t\leq(2\overline{\tau})^{-1}
, we have

|\log(1/\sigma_{t})|\leq\log(1/\sqrt{\underline{\tau}\delta})\leq\log(1/\delta),

(49)

where the first inequality holds by (
27
) and the last inequality holds with small enough
\delta
.
Combining (
46
) with (
48
), it follows that

\begin{split}&\left|\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2}}-f_{\rm pow}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})}\left(f_{\log}\left(f_{\sigma}(t)\right)\right)\right|\\
&\leq\delta+\left(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{\underline{\tau}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\underline{\tau}}}{2}\right)\sqrt{\delta}\left\{\log(1/\delta)\right\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}-\frac{1}{2})\tau_{\rm mult}}\\
&\leq D_{2}\sqrt{\delta}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}-\frac{1}{2})\tau_{\rm mult}}\end{split}

(50)

for
\delta\leq t\leq(2\overline{\tau})^{-1}
, where
D_{2}=1+(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(\frac{2}{\sqrt{\underline{\tau}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\underline{\tau}}}{2})
.
Combining (
47
)0 with the last two displays, we have

\displaystyle\left|\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2}}x-f_{\rm mult}^{(2)}\left(f_{\rm pow}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})}\left(f_{\log}\left(f_{\sigma}(t)\right)\right),x\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\delta+2D_{2}\sqrt{\delta}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})\tau_{\rm mult}}\leq D_{3}\sqrt{\delta}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})\tau_{\rm mult}}

for
\delta\leq t\leq(2\overline{\tau})^{-1}
and
|x|\leq 1
, where
D_{3}=1+2D_{2}
.
Let
m\in{n_{\beta}}{\mathbb{N}}
be a large enough value as described below.
Then, consider functions
g_{\rm y}^{(1)},\ldots,g_{\rm y}^{(m)}:[-1,1]\times[0,\infty)\to{\mathbb{R}}
and
f_{\rm y}^{(1)},\ldots,f_{\rm y}^{(m)}:{\mathbb{R}}\times{\mathbb{R}}\to{\mathbb{R}}
such that

\displaystyle g_{\rm y}^{(j)}(x,t)

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{2\tau_{\rm tail}}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2}}\left\{-x-\mu_{t}+\frac{\mu_{t}{n_{\beta}}}{m}\left(\widetilde{x}_{j-n_{\beta}\lfloor\frac{j}{{n_{\beta}}}\rfloor}+2\left\lfloor\frac{j}{{n_{\beta}}}\right\rfloor+1\right)\right\},\quad j\in[m]

for
x\in[-1,1],t\in[0,\infty)
and

\displaystyle f_{\rm y}^{(j)}(x,t)

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{2\tau_{\rm tail}}\left\{-f_{\rm mult}^{(2)}\left(f_{\rm pow}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})}\left(f_{\log}\left(f_{\sigma}(t)\right)\right),x\right)-f_{\rm mult}^{(2)}\left(f_{\rm pow}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})}\left(f_{\log}\left(f_{\sigma}(t)\right)\right),f_{\mu}(t)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\frac{{n_{\beta}}}{m}\left(\widetilde{x}_{j-n_{\beta}\lfloor\frac{j}{{n_{\beta}}}\rfloor}+2\left\lfloor\frac{j}{{n_{\beta}}}\right\rfloor+1\right)f_{\rm mult}^{(2)}\left(f_{\rm pow}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})}\left(f_{\log}\left(f_{\sigma}(t)\right)\right),f_{\mu}(t)\right)\right\},\quad j\in[m]

for
x,t\in{\mathbb{R}}
, where
\{(\widetilde{x}_{j},\widetilde{w}_{j}):j\in[{n_{\beta}}]\}
are the constants in Lemma
22
.
Then,

\displaystyle\left|g_{\rm y}^{(j)}(x,t)-f_{\rm y}^{(j)}(x,t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2\tau_{\rm tail}}\left\{D_{3}+\frac{D_{3}{n_{\beta}}}{m}\left(\left|\widetilde{x}_{j-n_{\beta}\lfloor\frac{j}{{n_{\beta}}}\rfloor}\right|+2\left\lfloor\frac{j}{{n_{\beta}}}\right\rfloor+1\right)+1\right\}\sqrt{\delta}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})\tau_{\rm mult}}

and

\displaystyle\left|g_{\rm y}^{(j)}(x,t)\right|\leq 2\sqrt{2\tau_{\rm tail}}\left\{2+\frac{2{n_{\beta}}}{m}\left(\left|\widetilde{x}_{j-n_{\beta}\lfloor\frac{j}{{n_{\beta}}}\rfloor}\right|+2\left\lfloor\frac{j}{{n_{\beta}}}\right\rfloor+1\right)\right\}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2}}

for
|x|\leq 1
and
\delta\leq t\leq(4\overline{\tau})^{-1}
, where the last inequality holds by (
49
).
Then, there exists a constant
D_{4}=D_{4}(\beta,\tau_{\rm tail},\tau_{\rm bd},D_{3})
such that

\begin{split}&\left|f_{\rm y}^{(j)}(x,t)\right|\leq\left|g_{\rm y}^{(j)}(x,t)-f_{\rm y}^{(j)}(x,t)\right|+\left|g_{\rm y}^{(j)}(x,t)\right|\leq D_{4}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2}},\quad\text{and}\\

\displaystyle\left|g_{\rm y}^{(j_{k})}(x_{k},t)p_{0}\left(\frac{{\bf x}+\sigma_{t}\left(g_{\rm y}^{(j_{1})}(x_{1},t),\ldots,g_{\rm y}^{(j_{D})}(x_{D},t)\right)^{\top}}{\mu_{t}}\right)\prod_{i=1}^{D}\phi\left(g_{\rm y}^{(j_{i})}(x_{i},t)\right)g_{\rm w}^{(j_{i})}(t)\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-\left({\bf f}_{\rm main}\left({\bf f}^{({\bf j})}({\bf x},t)\right)\right)_{k}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\delta+2D_{16}^{D}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{D(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})}\max\left\{\delta+3(K^{2}\vee 1)\left(\epsilon_{p_{0}}\vee\epsilon_{\phi}\right),\epsilon_{\rm w}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq D_{18}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{D(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})}\left[m^{-\frac{\beta}{d}}+{\delta}^{\frac{\beta\wedge 1}{2}}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+D+\frac{3}{2})\tau_{\rm mult}}\right],\quad k\in[D],\ j_{1},\ldots,j_{D}\in[m]

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}-\mu_{t}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}
and
\delta\leq t\leq\overline{\tau}^{-1}(\widetilde{C}_{2}^{2}\wedge 1/4)
, where

\displaystyle D_{18}=D_{18}(\beta,D,K,D_{8},D_{14},D_{15},D_{16}).

Let
\widetilde{C}_{1}
be the constant in Lemma
22
.
It follows that

\displaystyle\mu_{t}^{D}\left\|\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{{\bf j}\in[m]^{D}}{\bf f}_{\rm main}\left({\bf f}^{({\bf j})}({\bf x},t)\right)-\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})\\
p_{t}({\bf x})\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{C}_{1}K\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(D-1)}\left(\sigma_{t}^{\tau_{\rm tail}}+m^{-\beta}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(\beta+1)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+D_{18}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{D(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})}\left[m^{-\frac{\beta}{d}}+{\delta}^{\frac{\beta\wedge 1}{2}}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+D+\frac{3}{2})\tau_{\rm mult}}\right]

for
\delta\leq t\leq\overline{\tau}^{-1}(\widetilde{C}_{2}^{2}\wedge 1/2)
.
Let

\displaystyle m=n_{\beta}\lfloor\widetilde{m}\rfloor\quad\text{and}\quad\delta=\widetilde{m}^{-\tau_{\min}}

with large enough

.

, we have

for large enough

. Then,

\begin{split}&\left\|\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{{\bf j}\in[m]^{D}}{\bf f}_{\rm main}\left({\bf f}^{({\bf j})}({\bf x},t)\right)-\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})\\
p_{t}({\bf x})\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}\\
&\leq D_{19}\left(\log\widetilde{m}\right)^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(D-1)}\left\{t^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}}{2}}+\widetilde{m}^{-\frac{\beta}{d}}\left(\log\widetilde{m}\right)^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(\beta+1)}\right\},\end{split}

(61)

where

Recall that

and

Define a function

as

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bf f}_{\rm pre}({\bf x},t)=\rho\begin{pmatrix}{\bf f}_{\rm pre}({\bf x},t)\\
-{\bf f}_{\rm pre}({\bf x},t)\end{pmatrix}

for

and

.
For each

\displaystyle Q_{1}^{({\bf j})}\widetilde{\bf f}_{\rm pre}({\bf x},t)=\rho\begin{pmatrix}{\bf f}^{({\bf j})}({\bf x},t)\\
-{\bf f}^{({\bf j})}({\bf x},t)\\
\mathbf{0}_{6mD-6D}\end{pmatrix}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{6mD}.

x\vee 0-(-x\vee 0)=x

\displaystyle\widetilde{W}_{1}Q_{1}^{({\bf j})}\widetilde{\bf f}_{\rm pre}({\bf x},t)+\widetilde{\bf b}_{1}=\begin{pmatrix}W_{1}{\bf f}^{({\bf j})}({\bf x},t)+{\bf b}_{1}\\
\mathbf{0}_{m^{D}d_{2}-d_{2}}\end{pmatrix}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m^{D}d_{2}},\quad\forall{\bf j}\in[m]^{D},

\displaystyle\widetilde{W}_{1}=\begin{pmatrix}W_{1}\ &-W_{1}\ &\mathbf{0}\\

\mathbf{0}\end{pmatrix}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m^{D}d_{2}}.

\vskip 6.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 2.0pt\mathbf{0}_{m^{D}d_{2}-d_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{D}m^{i-1}(j_{i}-1)-d_{2}}\end{pmatrix}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m^{D}d_{2}}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{{\bf j}\in[m]^{D}}R_{1}^{({\bf j})}\left(\widetilde{W}_{1}Q_{1}^{({\bf j})}\widetilde{\bf f}_{\rm pre}({\bf x},t)+\widetilde{\bf b}_{1}\right)\right)_{d_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{D}m^{i-1}(\widetilde{j}_{i}-1)+1:d_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{D}m^{i-1}(\widetilde{j}_{i}-1)+d_{2}}

\displaystyle=W_{1}{\bf f}^{(\widetilde{\bf j})}({\bf x},t)+{\bf b}_{1},\quad\forall\widetilde{\bf j}=(\widetilde{j}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{j}_{D})\in[m]^{D}.

Let

,

-{\bf b}_{L_{\rm main}}\\

\displaystyle\quad=\rho\left(W_{L_{\rm main}}\cdot+{\bf b}_{L_{\rm main}}\right)\circ\rho\left(W_{L_{\rm main}-1}\cdot+{\bf b}_{L_{\rm main}-1}\right)\circ\cdots\circ\rho\left(W_{1}\cdot+{\bf b}_{1}\right)\circ{\bf f}^{({\bf j})}({\bf x},t)

\displaystyle\quad=\rho\left({\bf f}_{\rm main}\left({\bf f}^{({\bf j})}({\bf x},t)\right)\right)

Combining with (
61
), we have

\begin{split}&\left\|{\bf f}({\bf x},t)-\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})\\
p_{t}({\bf x})\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}\\
&\leq D_{19}\left(\log\widetilde{m}\right)^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(D-1)}\left\{t^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}}{2}}+\widetilde{m}^{-\frac{\beta}{d}}\left(\log\widetilde{m}\right)^{(\tau_{\rm bd}+\frac{1}{2})(\beta+1)}\right\}\end{split}

(62)

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}-\mu_{t}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}
and
\widetilde{m}^{-\tau_{\min}}\leq t\leq\overline{\tau}^{-1}(\widetilde{C}_{2}^{2}\wedge 1/2)
,
where

is a vector-valued function, defined as

\displaystyle{\bf f}({\bf x},t)=\begin{pmatrix}{\mathbb{I}}_{D+1}&\ -{\mathbb{I}}_{D+1}\end{pmatrix}{\bf f}_{1}({\bf x},t).

Note that
\|\widetilde{W}_{l}\|_{0}=\|W_{l}\|_{0}
,
\|\widetilde{\bf b}_{l}\|_{0}=\|{\bf b}_{l}\|_{0}
for

,

\|\widetilde{W}_{1}\|_{0}=2\|W_{1}\|_{0}
,
\|\widetilde{\bf b}_{1}\|_{0}=2\|{\bf b}_{1}\|_{0}
,

,

\|\widetilde{\bf b}_{L_{\rm main}}\|_{0}=2\|{\bf b}_{L_{\rm main}}\|_{0}
and
\|\widetilde{W}_{L_{\rm main}+1}\|_{0}=2D
.
Let

and

\displaystyle(\overline{d}_{1},\ldots,\overline{d}_{L+1})=(d_{\rm pre}^{(1)},\ldots,d_{\rm pre}^{(L_{\rm pre})},\widetilde{d}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{d}_{L_{\rm main}+2},d_{L_{\rm main}+1}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{i}=\left(Q_{i-L_{\rm pre}}^{({\bf j})}\right)_{{\bf j}\in[m]^{D}}\quad\text{and}\quad\mathcal{R}_{i}=\left(R_{i-L_{\rm pre}}^{({\bf j})}\right)_{{\bf j}\in[m]^{D}}.

for

Recall that

and

.

\displaystyle\overline{L}\leq D_{20}(\log\widetilde{m})^{2}\log\log\widetilde{m},\quad\|\overline{{\bf d}}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{20}\widetilde{m}^{D+1},

\displaystyle\overline{s}\leq D_{20}\widetilde{m}(\log\widetilde{m})^{5}\log\log\widetilde{m}^{D+1},\quad\overline{M}\leq\exp\left(D_{20}\{\log\widetilde{m}\}^{2}\right),

where

.
Combining (

) with the last display, the assertion follows by re-defining the constants.

B.3
Proof of Proposition
24

Proof.

Let
\tau_{\rm tail}=2\vee\sqrt{(D+3)/(2e)}
.
Given small enough
\delta>0
as described below, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\ p_{0}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}

\displaystyle\leq K\int_{\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ \phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}=K\mu_{t}^{-D}\int_{\|{\bf z}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\phi_{1}({\bf z}){\rm d}{\bf z}

\displaystyle\leq K\mu_{t}^{-D}\sum_{i=1}^{D}\int_{|z_{i}|\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\phi(z_{i}){\rm d}z_{i}\leq 2KD\mu_{t}^{-D}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/2}

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t>0
, where the last inequality holds by the tail probability of the standard normal distribution.
Also, for
i\in[D]
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y_{i}-x_{i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)p_{0}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq K\mu_{t}^{-D}\int_{\|{\bf z}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ \left|z_{i}\right|\phi_{1}({\bf z}){\rm d}{\bf z}\leq K\mu_{t}^{-D}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\int_{|z_{j}|\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ |z_{i}|\phi_{1}({\bf z}){\rm d}{\bf z}

\displaystyle=K\mu_{t}^{-D}\left\{(D-1){\mathbb{E}}[|Z|]\int_{|z|\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\phi(z){\rm d}z+\int_{|z|\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}|z|\phi(z){\rm d}z\right\}

\displaystyle\leq K\mu_{t}^{-D}\left\{2(D-1)\sqrt{2/\pi}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/2}+\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}[Z^{2}]}\sqrt{{\mathbb{P}}\left(|Z|\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq K\mu_{t}^{-D}\left\{2(D-1)\sqrt{2/\pi}+\sqrt{2}\right\}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/4},

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t>0
, where
Z
denotes the one-dimensional standard normal random variable and the second inequality holds by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.
Since
\mu_{t}^{-D}\leq 2^{D}
for
0\leq t\leq(2\overline{\tau})^{-1}
,

\begin{split}&\left|p_{t}({\bf x})-\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\ p_{0}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right|\leq KD2^{D+1}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/2},\\
&\left\|\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})-\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}{\bf y}-{\bf x}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)p_{0}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right\|_{\infty}\\
&\leq K2^{D}\left\{2(D-1)\sqrt{2/\pi}+\sqrt{2}\right\}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/4}\end{split}

(63)

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
0<t\leq(2\overline{\tau})^{-1}
.
For
0<t\leq(2\overline{\tau})^{-1}
and
{\bf x},{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
with
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\geq\mu_{t}-\tau_{\rm x}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}
and
\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\frac{\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}-\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}}{\mu_{t}}\geq 1-\left(\frac{\tau_{\rm x}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}{\mu_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\left[\tau_{\rm x}\left\{\log(1/\sqrt{2\overline{\tau}t})\right\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{2\overline{\tau}t\log(1/\delta)}\right],

where the last inequality holds by (
27
).
For
0<t\leq\delta^{\tau_{\rm t}}
and small enough
\delta
so that
\delta^{\tau_{\rm t}}\leq(2\overline{\tau})^{-1}
, the last display is bounded by

\displaystyle 1-2\left[\tau_{\rm x}\left\{\log(1/\sqrt{2\overline{\tau}\delta^{\tau_{\rm t}}})\right\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{2\overline{\tau}\delta^{\tau_{\rm t}}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right].

Moreover, the last display is lower bounded by
1-2D_{1}
for small enough
\delta
, where

\displaystyle D_{1}=\left(2\left\lfloor 2\left\{\log(1/\delta)\right\}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{\rm bd}}\vee 4\right\rfloor+2\right)^{-1}.

Then,
D_{1}\leq(\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{-\widetilde{\tau}_{\rm bd}}/2)\wedge(1/4)
and
D_{1}^{-1}\in 2{\mathbb{N}}
.
Let
{\bf y}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bf y}^{(D_{2})}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
be distinct vectors satisfying that

\displaystyle\left\{{\bf y}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bf y}^{(D_{2})}\right\}=\left\{D_{1}(n_{1},\ldots,n_{D})^{\top}:n_{i}\in{\mathbb{Z}},i\in[D]\right\}\cap\left\{{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}:\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}=1-D_{1}\right\},

where
D_{2}=(2/D_{1}-1)^{D}-(2/D_{1}-3)^{D}
.
Let
\mathcal{Y}_{i}=\{{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}:\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{1}\}
for
i\in[D_{2}]
.
For any
i\in[D_{2}]
and
{\bf y}\in\mathcal{Y}_{i}
, we have
1-2D_{1}\leq\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1
and
1-2D_{1}\leq\|{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq 1
.
Assume that the density
p_{0}
satisfies

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\alpha}\in{\mathbb{N}}^{D}}\sup_{1-\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{-\widetilde{\tau}_{\rm bd}}\leq\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq 1}\left|({\rm D}^{\bm{\alpha}}p_{0}({\bf x}))\right|\leq K.

With small enough
\delta
so that
D_{3}=\lfloor\log_{4/e}(1/\delta)\rfloor+1\geq 2
, Taylor’s theorem for multivariate function implies that

\displaystyle p_{0}({\bf y})=\sum_{0\leq k.<D_{3}}\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{0})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}+\sum_{k.=D_{3}}\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{0})(\xi{\bf y}^{(i)}+(1-\xi){\bf y})}{{\bf k}!}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}

for a suitable
\xi\in[0,1]
and
i\in[D_{2}],{\bf y}\in\mathcal{Y}_{i}
, where
({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}=\prod_{j=1}^{D}(y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j})^{k_{j}}
,
{\bf k}!=\prod_{j=1}^{D}k_{j}!
and
k.=\|{\bf k}\|_{1}
.
A simple calculation yields that

\displaystyle\left|p_{0}({\bf y})-\sum_{0\leq k.<D_{3}}\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{0})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}\right|\cdot 1\{\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{1}\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k.=D_{3}}K\prod_{j=1}^{D}\left(\frac{eD_{1}}{k_{j}}\right)^{k_{j}}\leq K(D_{3}+1)^{D}\left(\frac{e}{4}\right)^{D_{3}}

for
{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
i\in[D_{2}]
because
k!\geq k^{k}e^{-k}
for any
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geq 0}
and
0<D_{1}\leq 1/4
.
Since
D_{3}\geq\log\delta/\log(e/4)
,
(e/4)^{D_{3}}\leq\delta
.
Then, the last display is further bounded by

\displaystyle K\left(\lfloor\log_{4/e}(1/\delta)\rfloor+2\right)^{D}\delta\leq K3^{D}\delta\left\{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{\log(4/e)}\right\}^{D}.

Since
\mathcal{Y}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{Y}_{D_{2}}
are mutually disjoint except on a set of Lebesgue measure zero and
\bigcup_{i=1}^{D_{2}}\mathcal{Y}_{i}=\{{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}:1-2D_{1}\leq\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1\}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1\end{subarray}}g({\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}=\sum_{i=1}^{D_{2}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}g({\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}

\displaystyle=\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\sum_{i=1}^{D_{2}}g({\bf y})\cdot 1\{\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{1}\}{\rm d}{\bf y}

for any continuous function
g:{\mathbb{R}}^{D}\to{\mathbb{R}}
,
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\geq\mu_{t}-\tau_{\rm x}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}
and
0<t\leq\delta^{\tau_{\rm t}}
.
Combining (
63
) with the last two displays, we have

\displaystyle\left|p_{t}({\bf x})-\sum_{i=1}^{D_{2}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}\left\{\sum_{0\leq k.<D_{3}}\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{0})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}\right\}\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq KD2^{D+1}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/2}+K3^{D}\delta\left\{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{\log(e/4)}\right\}^{D}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

\displaystyle=KD2^{D+1}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/2}+K3^{D}\delta\left\{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{\log(4/e)}\right\}^{D}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}

for

and

Moreover, the last display is bounded by

Also, we have

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})-\sum_{i=1}^{D_{2}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle\qquad\left\{\sum_{0\leq k.<D_{3}}\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{0})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}\left(\frac{\mu_{t}{\bf y}-{\bf x}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}\right\}\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\Bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq K2^{D}\left\{2(D-1)\sqrt{2/\pi}+\sqrt{2}\right\}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/4}+K3^{D}\delta\left\{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{\log(4/e)}\right\}^{D}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\geq\mu_{t}-\tau_{\rm x}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-\tau_{\rm bd}}
and

.
Moreover, the last display is bounded by

(65)

For
x,y\in{\mathbb{R}}
and

, Taylor’s theorem yields that

\displaystyle\left|\exp\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)-\sum_{l=0}^{D_{4}-1}\frac{1}{l!}\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{l}\right|\leq\frac{1}{D_{4}!}\left(\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{D_{4}},

For

, the last display is further bounded by

Then,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-\mu_{t}y|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y-\widetilde{y}|\leq\widetilde{\tau}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y-x}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{m}(y-\widetilde{y})^{k}\exp\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right){\rm d}y\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-\sum_{l=0}^{D_{4}-1}\frac{1}{l!}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-\mu_{t}y|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-\mu_{t}y|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}\int_{|y|\leq 1}D_{1}^{k}\left\{\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right\}^{m}{\rm d}y\leq\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}\left\{\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right\}^{m}

,

Moreover,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{l=0}^{D_{4}-1}\frac{1}{l!}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-\mu_{t}y|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y-\widetilde{y}|\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y-x}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{m}(y-\widetilde{y})^{k}\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{l}{\rm d}y\right|

,

and

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-\mu_{t}y|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y-\widetilde{y}|\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y-x}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{m}(y-\widetilde{y})^{k}\exp\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right){\rm d}y\right|

\displaystyle\leq D_{1}^{k}\{\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\}^{m}\int_{|y|\leq 1}1{\rm d}y\leq\left\{\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right\}^{m}.

Note that
|\prod_{j=1}^{D}x_{j}-\prod_{j=1}^{D}\widetilde{x}_{j}|\leq DC^{D-1}\|{\bf x}-\widetilde{\bf x}\|_{\infty}
for any
{\bf x},\widetilde{\bf x}\in[-C,C]^{D}
.
Since the last two displays are bounded by
2\{\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\}^{m}
, we have

\begin{split}&\left|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y_{h}-x_{h}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{m}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}-\right.\\
&\prod_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{l=0}^{D_{4}-1}\left.\frac{1}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{t}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j}|\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y_{h}-x_{h}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{m\cdot 1\{h=j\}}(y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j})^{k_{j}}\left(-\frac{(x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{l}{\rm d}y_{j}\right|\\
&\leq(2\pi\sigma_{t}^{2})^{-\frac{D}{2}}D2^{D-1}\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}\left\{\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right\}^{mD}\end{split}

(66)

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D},t\geq 0,h\in[D],i\in[D_{2}],j\in[D],{\bf k}\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geq 0}^{D}
and

with small enough
\delta
so that

With

in the last display, the last integral satisfies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{t}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j}|\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}\ \left(y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j}\right)^{k_{j}}\left(-\frac{(x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{l}{\rm d}y_{j}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mu_{t}^{-1}}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|\mu_{t}^{-1}\sigma_{t}z_{j}+\mu_{t}^{-1}x_{j}-y_{j}^{(i)}|\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\mu_{t}^{-1}\sigma_{t}z_{j}+\mu_{t}^{-1}x_{j}-y_{j}^{(i)}\right)^{k_{j}}z_{j}^{2l}(-2)^{-l}{\rm d}z_{j}

\displaystyle=\frac{(-2)^{-l}\mu_{t}^{-1}}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|\mu_{t}^{-1}\sigma_{t}z_{j}+\mu_{t}^{-1}x_{j}-y_{j}^{(i)}|\leq D_{1}\end{subarray}}\ \sum_{r_{j}=0}^{k_{j}}\binom{k_{j}}{r_{j}}\left(\mu_{t}^{-1}\sigma_{t}\right)^{r_{j}}\left(\mu_{t}^{-1}x_{j}-y_{j}^{(i)}\right)^{k_{j}-r_{j}}z_{j}^{r_{j}+2l}\ {\rm d}z_{j}

\displaystyle=\frac{(-2)^{-l}\mu_{t}^{-k_{j}-1}}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}}\sum_{r_{j}=0}^{k_{j}}\binom{k_{j}}{r_{j}}\sigma_{t}^{r_{j}}\left(x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j}^{(i)}\right)^{k_{j}-r_{j}}\left(\frac{\overline{z}_{i,j}^{r_{j}+2l+1}-\underline{z}_{i,j}^{r_{j}+2l+1}}{r_{j}+2l+1}\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}P_{i,j,k_{j},l}(x_{j},t),

where

\displaystyle\overline{z}_{i,j}=\min\left(\max\left(\frac{\mu_{t}(y_{j}^{(i)}+D_{1})-x_{j}}{\sigma_{t}},-\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right),\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right),

\displaystyle\underline{z}_{i,j}=\min\left(\max\left(\frac{\mu_{t}(y_{j}^{(i)}-D_{1})-x_{j}}{\sigma_{t}},-\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right),\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right).

Combining (
27
), (
64
) and (
66
) with the last two displays, we have

\displaystyle\left|p_{t}({\bf x})-g_{t}({\bf x})\right|

\displaystyle\leq KD2^{D+1}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/2}+K6^{D}\delta\left\{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{\log(4/e)}\right\}^{D}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{D_{2}}\sum_{0\leq k.<D_{3}}\left|\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{0})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}\right|(2\pi\sigma_{t}^{2})^{-\frac{D}{2}}D2^{D-1}\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq D_{5}\left[\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/2}+\delta\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{D}+\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}-D/2}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{D\widetilde{\tau}_{\rm bd}+D}\right]

for

Since

Similarly, with

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{t}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

\displaystyle=\frac{\mu_{t}^{-1}}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

Combining (

) with the last display, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sigma_{t}\left(\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})\right)_{h}-\widetilde{g}_{t}^{(h)}({\bf x})\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{D_{2}}\sum_{0\leq k.<D_{3}}\left|\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{0})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}\right|(2\pi\sigma_{t}^{2})^{-\frac{D}{2}}D2^{D-1}\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}\left\{\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right\}^{D}

for

j\neq h\end{subarray}}^{D}\sum_{l=0}^{D_{4}-1}\frac{P_{i,j,k_{j},l}(x_{j},t)}{k_{j}!}\right\}\left\{\sum_{l=0}^{D_{4}-1}\frac{\widetilde{P}_{i,h,k_{h},l}(x_{h},t)}{k_{h}!}\right\}.

\left|\sigma_{t}\left(\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})\right)_{h}-\widetilde{g}_{t}^{(h)}({\bf x})\right|\leq 3D_{6}\delta\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{D},\quad h\in[D]

(68)

for small enough
\delta
.

Let
0<\widetilde{\delta}<\delta
be a small enough value as described below.
With
{\widetilde{\delta}}^{2}<1/2
, Lemma
18
implies that
there exist neural networks
f_{\mu}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\mu},{\bf d}_{\mu},s_{\mu},M_{\mu}),f_{\sigma}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\sigma},{\bf d}_{\sigma},s_{\sigma},M_{\sigma})
with

\displaystyle L_{\mu},L_{\sigma}\leq C_{N,4}\{\log(1/\widetilde{\delta})\}^{2},\quad\|{\bf d}_{\mu}\|_{\infty},\|{\bf d}_{\sigma}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{N,4}\{\log(1/\widetilde{\delta})\}^{2}

\displaystyle s_{\mu},s_{\sigma}\leq C_{N,4}\{\log(1/\widetilde{\delta})\}^{3},\quad M_{\mu},M_{\sigma}\leq C_{N,4}\log(1/\widetilde{\delta})

such that
|\mu_{t}-f_{\mu}(t)|\leq\widetilde{\delta}
and
|\sigma_{t}-f_{\sigma}(t)|\leq\widetilde{\delta}

for
t\geq\widetilde{\delta}
, where
C_{N,4}
is the constant in Lemma
18
.
Also, Lemma
19
implies that there exist a neural network
f_{\rm rec}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\rm rec},{\bf d}_{\rm rec},s_{\rm rec},M_{\rm rec})
with

\displaystyle L_{\rm rec}\leq C_{N,5}\{\log(1/\widetilde{\delta})\}^{2},\quad\|{\bf d}_{\rm rec}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{N,5}\{\log(1/\widetilde{\delta})\}^{3}

\begin{split}&\left|1/\sigma_{t}-f_{\rm rec}(f_{\sigma}(t))\right|\leq\left|1/\sigma_{t}-1/f_{\sigma}(t)\right|+\left|1/f_{\sigma}(t)-f_{\rm rec}(f_{\sigma}(t))\right|\\

(69)

for

Lemma

\displaystyle\widetilde{f}_{\rm mult}^{(k)}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(\widetilde{L}_{\rm mult}^{(k)},\widetilde{\bf d}_{\rm mult}^{(k)},\widetilde{s}_{\rm mult}^{(k)},\widetilde{M}_{\rm mult}^{(k)}),\quad k\geq 2

B.4
Proof of Proposition
25

Proof.

Let

\displaystyle\tau_{\rm t}=\overline{\tau}^{-1}\quad\text{and}\quad\tau_{\rm tail}=\left\{4(D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}+1)\vee\left(\frac{D+1}{e}\right)\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Let
t_{*}>0
and
0<\delta<1
be small enough values as described below.
By the Markov property of
({\bf X}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
, we have

\displaystyle p_{t_{*}+t}({\bf x})=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{D}}p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}

for any
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t\geq 0
.
Let
C_{S,1}=C_{S,1}(D,K,\tau_{1})
be the constant in Lemma
6
. Since
|p_{t_{*}}({\bf x})|\leq C_{S,1}
for any
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}

\displaystyle\leq C_{S,1}\int_{\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ \phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}=C_{S,1}\mu_{t}^{-D}\int_{\|{\bf z}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\phi_{1}({\bf z}){\rm d}{\bf z}

\displaystyle\leq C_{S,1}\mu_{t}^{-D}\sum_{i=1}^{D}\int_{|z_{i}|\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\phi(z_{i}){\rm d}z_{i}\leq 2C_{S,1}D\mu_{t}^{-D}\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}}

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t\geq 0
, where the last inequality holds by the tail probability of the standard normal distribution.
Also,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y_{i}-x_{i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{S,1}\mu_{t}^{-D}\int_{\|{\bf z}\|_{\infty}\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ \left|z_{i}\right|\phi_{1}({\bf z}){\rm d}{\bf z}\leq C_{S,1}\mu_{t}^{-D}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\int_{|z_{i}|\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ |z_{i}|\phi_{1}({\bf z}){\rm d}{\bf z}

\displaystyle=C_{S,1}\mu_{t}^{-D}\left\{(D-1){\mathbb{E}}[|Z|]\int_{|z|\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\phi(z){\rm d}z+\int_{|z|\geq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}|z|\phi(z){\rm d}z\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{S,1}\mu_{t}^{-D}\left\{(D-1)\sqrt{2/\pi}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/2}+\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}[Z^{2}]}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/4}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{S,1}D\mu_{t}^{-D}\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{4}},\quad i\in[D]

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t\geq 0
, where
Z
denotes the one-dimensional standard normal random variable and the second inequality holds by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.
Note that
\exp(-\overline{\tau}t)\leq\mu_{t}\leq\exp(-\underline{\tau}t)
for
t\geq 0
.
Then,

\begin{split}&\left|p_{t_{*}+t}({\bf x})-\int_{\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right|\leq 2C_{S,1}D\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}-D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}},\\
&\left\|\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t_{*}+t}({\bf x})-\int_{\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}{\bf y}-{\bf x}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{\top}p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right\|_{\infty}\\
&\leq 2C_{S,1}D\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{4}-D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}}\end{split}

(74)

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
0\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm t}\log(1/\delta)
.
Lemma
6
implies that
p_{t_{*}}({\bf x})\leq C_{S,1}\delta^{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}/2}
for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\geq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}
. Then,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\end{subarray}}p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{S,1}\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\end{subarray}}\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}

\displaystyle\leq C_{S,1}\mu_{t}^{-D}\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{D}}\phi_{1}({\bf z}){\rm d}{\bf z}\leq C_{S,1}\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}-D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}}

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
0\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm t}\log(1/\delta)
, where the last inequaltiy holds because
\mu_{t}^{-D}\leq\exp(D\overline{\tau}t)
.
Also,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\geq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{\mu_{t}y_{i}-x_{i}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{S,1}\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}}\mu_{t}^{-D}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{D}}|z_{i}|\phi_{1}({\bf z}){\rm d}{\bf z}\leq\left(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\right)C_{S,1}\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}-D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}}

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
0\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm t}\log(1/\delta)
.
Combining with (
74
), we have

\begin{split}&\left|p_{t_{*}+t}({\bf x})-\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\end{subarray}}\ p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right|\\
&\leq C_{S,1}(2D+1)\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}-D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}}\end{split}

(75)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t_{*}+t}({\bf x})-\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}{\bf y}-{\bf x}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{\top}p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{S,1}(2D+\sqrt{2/\pi})\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{4}-D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}}

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
0\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm t}\log(1/\delta)
.
Let
m_{*}\in{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq 2}
be a large enough value as described below and
{\bf y}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bf y}^{(D_{1})}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
be distinct vectors satisfying that

\displaystyle\left\{{\bf y}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bf y}^{(D_{1})}\right\}=\left\{\tau_{*}(n_{1},\ldots,n_{D})^{\top}:n_{i}\in{\mathbb{Z}},i\in[D]\right\}\cap\left\{{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}:\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq(m_{*}-1)\tau_{*}\right\},

where
\tau_{*}=\{\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\}/m_{*}
and
D_{1}=(2m_{*}-1)^{D}
. Let
\mathcal{Y}_{i}=\{{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}:\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{*}\}
for
i\in[D_{1}]
.
Taylor’s theorem for multivariate function implies that

\displaystyle p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})=\sum_{0\leq k.<\tau_{\rm sm}}\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{t_{*}})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}+\sum_{k.=\tau_{\rm sm}}\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{t_{*}})(\xi{\bf y}^{(i)}+(1-\xi){\bf y})}{{\bf k}!}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}

for a suitable
\xi\in[0,1]
and
i\in[D_{1}],{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
, where
({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}=\prod_{j=1}^{D}(y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j})^{k_{j}}
,
{\bf k}!=\prod_{j=1}^{D}k_{j}!
and
k.=\|{\bf k}\|_{1}
.
Combining with Lemma
8
, we have

\displaystyle\left|p_{t_{*}}({\bf y})-\sum_{0\leq k.<\tau_{\rm sm}}\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{t_{*}})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}\right|\cdot 1\{\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{*}\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k.=\tau_{\rm sm}}C_{S,3}\sigma_{t_{*}}^{-\tau_{\rm sm}}\prod_{j=1}^{D}\left(\frac{e\tau_{*}}{k_{j}}\right)^{k_{j}}\leq C_{S,3}(\tau_{\rm sm}+1)^{D}\left(\frac{e\tau_{*}}{\sigma_{t_{*}}}\right)^{\tau_{\rm sm}}

for
{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
i\in[D_{1}]
because
k!\geq k^{k}e^{-k}
for any
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geq 0}
, where
C_{S,3}=C_{S,3}(D,K,\tau_{\rm sm},\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau})
is the constant in Lemma
8
.
Since
\mathcal{Y}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{Y}_{D_{1}}
are mutually disjoint except on a set of Lebesgue measure zero and
\bigcup_{i=1}^{D_{1}}\mathcal{Y}_{i}=\{{\bf y}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}:\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\}
,
we have

\displaystyle\int_{\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ g({\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}=\sum_{i=1}^{D_{1}}\int_{\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{*}}g({\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}

\displaystyle=\int_{\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}}\ \sum_{i=1}^{D_{1}}g({\bf y})\cdot 1\{\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{*}\}{\rm d}{\bf y}

for any continuous function
g:{\mathbb{R}}^{D}\to{\mathbb{R}}
.
Combining (
75
) with the last two displays, we have

\begin{split}&\left|p_{t_{*}+t}({\bf x})-\sum_{i=1}^{D_{1}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{*}\end{subarray}}\left\{\sum_{0\leq k.<\tau_{\rm sm}}\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{t_{*}})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}\right\}\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}\right|\\
&\leq C_{S,1}(2D+1)\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}-D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}}\\
&\quad+C_{S,3}(\tau_{\rm sm}+1)^{D}\left(\frac{e\tau_{*}}{\sigma_{t_{*}}}\right)^{\tau_{\rm sm}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\end{subarray}}\ \sum_{i=1}^{D_{1}}\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y})\cdot 1\{\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{*}\}{\rm d}{\bf y}\\

(76)

for

and

, where the last inequality holds because

.
Also, we have

\begin{split}&\left\|\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t_{*}+t}({\bf x})-\sum_{i=1}^{D_{1}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

&\qquad\cdot\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t_{*}}+\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t_{*}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\end{subarray}}\sum_{i=1}^{D_{1}}\left\|\frac{\mu_{t}{\bf y}-{\bf x}}{\sigma_{t}}\right\|_{\infty}\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y})\cdot 1\{\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{*}\}{\rm d}{\bf y}\\

(77)

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and

, where the last inequality holds because

.

For
x,y\in{\mathbb{R}}
and
t>0
, Taylor’s theorem yields that

\displaystyle\left|\exp\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)-\sum_{l=0}^{D_{2}-1}\frac{1}{l!}\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{l}\right|\leq\frac{1}{D_{2}!}\left(\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{D_{2}},

For

Then,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-\mu_{t}y|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y-\widetilde{y}|\leq\tau_{*}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y-x}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{u}(y-\widetilde{y})^{k}\exp\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right){\rm d}y\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-\sum_{l=0}^{D_{2}-1}\frac{1}{l!}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-\mu_{t}y|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

for any

with

,

and

.
Note that

For

, the last display is bounded by

Moreover,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{l=0}^{D_{2}-1}\frac{1}{l!}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-\mu_{t}y|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y-\widetilde{y}|\leq\tau_{*}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y-x}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{u}(y-\widetilde{y})^{k}\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{l}{\rm d}y\right|

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\left(1+\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}\right)(1+\tau_{\rm tail})^{\tau_{\rm sm}+1}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{\frac{\tau_{\rm sm}}{2}+1}

with

,

and

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-\mu_{t}y|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y-\widetilde{y}|\leq\tau_{*}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y-x}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{u}(y-\widetilde{y})^{k}\exp\left(-\frac{(x-\mu_{t}y)^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right){\rm d}y\right|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{|y|\leq m_{*}\tau_{*}}\left\{\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right\}^{u}\tau_{*}^{k}{\rm d}y\leq m_{*}\tau_{*}^{k+1}\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{\rm tail}(1+\tau_{\rm tail})^{\tau_{\rm sm}+1}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{\frac{\tau_{\rm sm}}{2}+1}.

Note that
|\prod_{j=1}^{D}x_{j}-\prod_{j=1}^{D}\widetilde{x}_{j}|\leq DC^{D-1}\|{\bf x}-\widetilde{\bf x}\|_{\infty}

Since the last two displays are bounded by

we have

\begin{split}&\left|\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|{\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
\|{\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)}\|_{\infty}\leq\tau_{*}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y_{h}-x_{h}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{u}({\bf y}-{\bf y}^{(i)})^{{\bf k}}\phi_{\sigma_{t}}({\bf x}-\mu_{t}{\bf y}){\rm d}{\bf y}-\right.\\
&\prod_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{l=0}^{D_{2}-1}\left.\frac{1}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{t}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j}|\leq\tau_{*}\end{subarray}}\ \left(\frac{\mu_{t}y_{h}-x_{h}}{\sigma_{t}}\right)^{u\cdot 1\{h=j\}}(y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j})^{k_{j}}\left(-\frac{(x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{l}{\rm d}y_{j}\right|\\
&\leq(2\pi\sigma_{t}^{2})^{-\frac{D}{2}}D2^{D-1}\tau_{\rm tail}^{D}(1+\tau_{\rm tail})^{D(\tau_{\rm sm}+1)}\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{D(\frac{\tau_{\rm sm}}{2}+1)}\end{split}

(78)

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D},t>0,h\in[D],i\in[D_{1}],j\in[D],{\bf k}\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geq 0}^{D}
and

.
With

in the last display, the second integral satisfies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}\sigma_{t}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sigma_{t}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j}|\leq\tau_{*}\end{subarray}}\ \left(y_{j}-y^{(i)}_{j}\right)^{k_{j}}\left(-\frac{(x_{j}-\mu_{t}y_{j})^{2}}{2\sigma_{t}^{2}}\right)^{l}{\rm d}y_{j}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mu_{t}^{-1}}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

\displaystyle=\frac{(-2)^{-l}\mu_{t}^{-1}}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\
|\mu_{t}^{-1}\sigma_{t}z_{j}+\mu_{t}^{-1}x_{j}-y_{j}^{(i)}|\leq\tau_{*}\end{subarray}}\ \sum_{r_{j}=0}^{k_{j}}\binom{k_{j}}{r_{j}}\left(\mu_{t}^{-1}\sigma_{t}\right)^{r_{j}}\left(\mu_{t}^{-1}x_{j}-y_{j}^{(i)}\right)^{k_{j}-r_{j}}z_{j}^{r_{j}+2l}\ {\rm d}z_{j}

where

\displaystyle\overline{z}_{i,j}=\min\left(\max\left(\frac{\mu_{t}(y_{j}^{(i)}+\tau_{*})-x_{j}}{\sigma_{t}},-\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right),\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right),

\displaystyle\underline{z}_{i,j}=\min\left(\max\left(\frac{\mu_{t}(y_{j}^{(i)}-\tau_{*})-x_{j}}{\sigma_{t}},-\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right),\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\right).

Combining (
76
) and (
78
) with the last two displays, we have

\displaystyle\left|p_{t_{*}+t}({\bf x})-g_{t_{*},t}({\bf x})\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{S,1}(2D+1)\delta^{\frac{\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}{2}-D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}}+C_{S,3}(\tau_{\rm sm}+1)^{D}\left(\frac{e\tau_{*}}{\sigma_{t_{*}}}\right)^{\tau_{\rm sm}}\delta^{-D\overline{\tau}\tau_{\rm t}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{D_{1}}\sum_{0\leq k.<\tau_{\rm sm}}\left|\frac{({\rm D}^{{\bf k}}p_{t_{*}})({\bf y}^{(i)})}{{\bf k}!}\right|(2\pi\sigma_{t}^{2})^{-\frac{D}{2}}D2^{D-1}\tau_{\rm tail}^{D}(1+\tau_{\rm tail})^{D(\tau_{\rm sm}+1)}\delta^{e\tau_{\rm tail}^{2}}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{D(\frac{\tau_{\rm sm}}{2}+1)}

for

and

, where

Let

\begin{split}&\left|p_{t_{*}+t}({\bf x})-g_{t_{*},t}({\bf x})\right|\\

(79)

Similarly, with

\displaystyle=\frac{\mu_{t}^{-1}}{l!\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z_{j}|\leq\tau_{\rm tail}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}\\

Combining (

.
Consider a function
{\bf f}_{t_{*}}:{\mathbb{R}}^{D}\times{\mathbb{R}}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{D+1}
such that

{\bf f}_{t_{*}}({\bf x},t)=(f_{t_{*}}({\bf x},t),\widetilde{f}_{t_{*}}^{(1)}({\bf x},t),\ldots,\widetilde{f}_{t_{*}}^{(D)}({\bf x},t))^{\top}
for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t\in{\mathbb{R}}
. Lemma
9
, Lemma
10
, Lemma
11
and Lemma
12
implies that
{\bf f}_{t_{*}}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L,{\bf d},s,m)
with

\displaystyle L\leq D_{9}(\log m)^{4},\quad\|{\bf d}\|_{\infty}\leq D_{9}m(\log m)^{9},

\displaystyle s\leq D_{9}m(\log m)^{9},\quad M\leq\exp(D_{9}(\log m)^{2}),

where
D_{9}=D_{9}(D,\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau},\tau_{\rm t},\tau_{\rm x},\tau_{\rm sm},\tau_{\rm low},\tau_{\rm tail},C_{S,3},C_{N,4},C_{N,5},D_{7},D_{8})
is a large enough constant.
The assertion follows by re-defining the constants.

∎

B.5
Proof of Theorem
5

In this subsection, we provide the proof of Theorem
5
by combining Propositions
23
to
25
.

Proof of Theorem
5
.

Let
m>0
be a large enough value as described below and

\displaystyle D_{1}=\sqrt{\frac{8\beta}{d}}\vee\sqrt{2\tau_{\rm min}+\frac{4\beta}{d}}.

We will approximate
\nabla\log p_{t}({\bf x})
for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}+\sigma_{t}D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}
and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm max}\log m
by neural networks by dividing the analysis into the following three cases:

1.

(Interior of near-support)
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}-\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-3/2}
and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}

2.

(Boundary of near-support)
\mu_{t}-\tau_{\rm min}^{3/2}\{(4D)^{3/2}+3\}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-3/2}\leq\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}+\sigma_{t}D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}

and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}

3.

(large
t
)
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}+\sigma_{t}D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}
and
2m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm max}\log m
.

Then, we combine the networks into a single network and derive the approximation error over the entire region
({\bf x},t)\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}\times[m^{-\tau_{\rm min}},\tau_{\rm max}\log m]
.

B.5.1
Interior of Near-Support

Let
\tau_{\rm tail}^{(1)}=4D\beta/d
and
\tau_{\rm bd}^{(1)}=3/2
.
Let

\displaystyle\widetilde{C}_{3}=\widetilde{C}_{3}(\beta,d,D,K,\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau},\tau_{\rm bd}^{(1)},\tau_{\rm tail}^{(1)},\tau_{\rm min}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{C}_{4}=\widetilde{C}_{4}(\beta,d,D,K,\underline{\tau},\tau_{\rm bd}^{(1)},\tau_{\rm tail}^{(1)},\tau_{\rm min}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{C}_{5}=\widetilde{C}_{5}(\beta,d,\underline{\tau},\tau_{\rm bd}^{(1)},\tau_{\rm tail}^{(1)},\tau_{\min})

be the constants in Proposition
23
, where
(\tau_{\rm tail},\tau_{\rm bd})
is replaced by
(\tau_{\rm tail}^{(1)},\tau_{\rm bd}^{(1)})
.
Also, let
\widetilde{C}_{2}=\widetilde{C}_{2}(\beta,D,\tau_{\rm bd}^{(1)},\tau_{\rm tail}^{(1)})
be the constant in Lemma
22
, where
(\tau_{\rm tail},\tau_{\rm bd})
is replaced by
(\tau_{\rm tail}^{(1)},\tau_{\rm bd}^{(1)})
.
For large enough
m
so that
m\geq\widetilde{C}_{5}
and
3m^{-1/(2D)}\leq\overline{\tau}^{-1}(\widetilde{C}_{2}^{2}\wedge 1/2)
, Proposition
23
implies that
there exists a collection of permutation matrices
\mathcal{P}^{(1)}=(\mathcal{Q}_{i}^{(1)},\mathcal{R}_{i}^{(1)})_{i\in[L-1]}
and weight-sharing neural networks

\displaystyle{\bf f}^{(1)}=(f_{1}^{(1)},\ldots,f_{D+1}^{(1)})^{\top}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L^{(1)},{\bf d}^{(1)},s^{(1)},M^{(1)},\mathcal{P}^{(1)}_{{\bf m}^{(1)}})

with

\displaystyle L^{(1)}\leq\widetilde{C}_{3}(\log m)^{2}\log\log m,\quad\|{\bf d}^{(1)}\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{C}_{3}m^{D+1},

\displaystyle s^{(1)}\leq\widetilde{C}_{3}m(\log m)^{5}\log\log m,\quad M^{(1)}\leq\exp(\widetilde{C}_{3}\{\log m\}^{2}),

\displaystyle\|{\bf m}^{(1)}\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{C}_{3}m^{D}

satisfying

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})\\
p_{t}({\bf x})\end{pmatrix}-{\bf f}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{C}_{4}\left(3^{\frac{2D\beta}{d}}+1\right)m^{-\frac{\beta}{d}}(\log m)^{2D+2\beta}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}\epsilon_{1}

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}-\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-3/2}
and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}
because
\mu_{t}\leq 1
.
Note that
C_{S,1}^{-1}\leq p_{t}({\bf x})\leq C_{S,1}
for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}
,
t\geq 0
, and
\|\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})\|_{\infty}\leq C_{S,3}
for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D},t\geq 0
, where
C_{S,1}=C_{S,1}(D,K,\tau_{1})
and
C_{S,3}=C_{S,3}(D,K,\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau})
are the constants in Lemma
6
and Lemma
8
, respectively.
Also,
C_{S,1}^{-1}/2\leq p_{t}({\bf x})-\epsilon_{1}\leq f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\leq p_{t}({\bf x})+\epsilon_{1}\leq 2C_{S,1}
for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}-\mu_{t}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-3/2}
and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}
with large enough
m
so that
\epsilon_{1}\leq C_{S,1}^{-1}/2
.
Let
f_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)},{\bf d}_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)},s_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)},M_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)})
be the neural networks in Lemma
19
with

\displaystyle L_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)}\leq C_{N,5}\{\beta\log m/d\}^{2},\quad\|{\bf d}_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{N,5}\{\beta\log m/d\}^{3},

\displaystyle s_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)}\leq C_{N,5}\{\beta\log m/d\}^{4},\quad M_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)}\leq C_{N,5}m^{\frac{2\beta}{d}}

such that
|1/x-f_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)}(x)|\leq m^{-\beta/d}
for
x\in[m^{-\beta/d},m^{\beta/d}]
.
For large enough
m
so that
m^{-\beta/d}\leq C_{S,1}^{-1}/2
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-f_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right|\leq\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-\frac{1}{f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)}-f_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\{p_{t}({\bf x})\wedge f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\}^{-2}\epsilon_{1}+m^{-\frac{\beta}{d}}\leq(4C_{S,1}^{2}+1)\epsilon_{1}

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}-\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-3/2}
and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}
.
Let

\displaystyle f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)},{\bf d}_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)},s_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)},M_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)})

be the neural networks in Lemma
14
with

\displaystyle L_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}\leq C_{N,1}\log 2\{\beta\log m/d+2\log(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})+2\log 2\},\quad{\bf d}_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}=(2,96,\ldots,96,1)^{\top},

\displaystyle s_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}\leq C_{N,1}2\{\beta\log m/d+\log(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})+\log 2\},\quad M_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}=(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})^{2}

such that
|f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}(\widetilde{\bf x})-x_{1}x_{2}|\leq m^{-\beta/d}+2(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})\widetilde{\epsilon}
for all
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq 2(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})
,
\widetilde{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
with
\|{\bf x}-\widetilde{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{\epsilon}
and
|f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}({\bf x})|\leq(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})^{2}
for all
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
, where
C_{N,1}
is the constant in Lemma
14
.
Then,

\begin{split}&\left|\frac{\sigma_{t}(\nabla p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{i}^{(1)}({\bf x},t),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right)\right|\\
&\leq m^{-\frac{\beta}{d}}+2(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})(4C_{S,1}^{2}+1)\epsilon_{1}\leq D_{2}m^{-\frac{\beta}{d}}(\log m)^{2D+2\beta},\quad i\in[D]\end{split}

(88)

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}-\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-3/2}
and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}
, where
D_{2}=D_{2}(\beta,D,C_{S,1},C_{S,3},\widetilde{C}_{4})
.
Let
0<\delta\leq\underline{T}=m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}
be a small enough value as described below.
With
{\delta}<1/2
, Lemma
18
implies that
there exist neural networks
f_{\sigma}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\sigma},{\bf d}_{\sigma},s_{\sigma},M_{\sigma})
with

\begin{split}&L_{\sigma}\leq C_{N,4}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{2},\quad\|{\bf d}_{\sigma}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{N,4}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{2}\\
&s_{\sigma}\leq C_{N,4}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{3},\quad M_{\sigma}\leq C_{N,4}\log(1/\delta)\end{split}

(89)

such that
|\sigma_{t}-f_{\sigma}(t)|\leq\delta

for
t\geq\delta
, where
C_{N,4}
is the constant in Lemma
18
.
Also, Lemma
19
implies that there exists a neural network
f_{\rm rec}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\rm rec},{\bf d}_{\rm rec},s_{\rm rec},M_{\rm rec})
with

\displaystyle L_{\rm rec}\leq C_{N,5}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{2},\quad\|{\bf d}_{\rm rec}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{N,5}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{3}

\displaystyle s_{\rm rec}\leq C_{N,5}\{\log(1/\delta)\}^{4},\quad M_{\rm rec}\leq C_{N,5}\delta^{-2}

such that
|1/x-f_{\rm rec}(x)|\leq\delta
for any
x\in[\delta,1/\delta]
.
Since
\sigma_{t}-\delta\leq f_{\sigma}(t)\leq\sigma_{t}+\delta
for
t\geq\delta
and
\sqrt{\underline{\tau}\underline{T}}\leq\sigma_{t}\leq 1
for
t\geq\underline{T}
, we have
\underline{T}\leq f_{\sigma}(t)\leq 2
for
t\geq\underline{T}
with large enough
m
so that
\underline{T}\leq\sqrt{\underline{\tau}\underline{T}}-\underline{T}
and
\underline{T}\leq 1
.
Then,

\begin{split}&\left|1/\sigma_{t}-f_{\rm rec}(f_{\sigma}(t))\right|\leq\left|1/\sigma_{t}-1/f_{\sigma}(t)\right|+\left|1/f_{\sigma}(t)-f_{\rm rec}(f_{\sigma}(t))\right|\\
&\leq\{\sigma_{t}\wedge f_{\sigma}(t)\}^{-2}|\sigma_{t}-f_{\sigma}(t)|+\delta\leq(1+\underline{T}^{-2})\delta=(1+m^{2\tau_{\rm min}})\delta\end{split}

(90)

for
t\geq\underline{T}
.
Lemma
14
implies that there exists a neural network

\displaystyle f_{\rm mult}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{\rm mult},{\bf d}_{\rm mult},s_{\rm mult},M_{\rm mult})

with

\displaystyle L_{\rm mult}\leq C_{N,1}\log 2\{\log(1/\delta)+3DD_{1}^{2}\log m\},\quad{\bf d}_{\rm mult}=(2,96,\ldots,96,1)^{\top},

\displaystyle s_{\rm mult}\leq C_{N,1}2\{\log(1/\delta)+3DD_{1}^{2}\log m\},\quad M_{\rm mult}=m^{6DD_{1}^{2}}

such that

\left|f_{\rm mult}(\widetilde{x}_{1},\widetilde{x}_{2})-x_{1}x_{2}\right|\leq\delta+2m^{3DD_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{\epsilon}

(91)

for any
{\bf x}=(x_{1},x_{2})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
with
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq m^{3DD_{1}^{2}}
and
\widetilde{\bf x}=(\widetilde{x}_{1},\widetilde{x}_{2})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k}
with
\|{\bf x}-\widetilde{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{\epsilon}
,
where
0<\widetilde{\epsilon}\leq 1
.
Consider functions
\widetilde{f}_{1}^{(1)},\ldots,\widetilde{f}_{D}^{(1)}:{\mathbb{R}}^{D}\times{\mathbb{R}}\to{\mathbb{R}}
such that

\displaystyle\widetilde{f}^{(1)}_{i}({\bf x},t)=f_{\rm mult}\left(f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{i}^{(1)}({\bf x},t),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right),f_{\rm rec}(f_{\sigma}(t))\right),\quad i\in[D]

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t\in{\mathbb{R}}
.
For large enough
m
so that

, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sigma_{t}^{-1}f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{i}^{(1)}({\bf x},t),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right)-\widetilde{f}^{(1)}_{i}({\bf x},t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\delta+2m^{3DD_{1}^{2}}(1+m^{2\tau_{\rm min}})\delta\leq 5m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta,\quad i\in[D]

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t\geq\underline{T}
because
D_{1}^{2}\geq\tau_{\rm min}
.
Combining (
88
) with the last display, we have

\begin{split}&\left|(\nabla\log p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}-\widetilde{f}_{i}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\right|\sigma_{t}\\
&\leq\left|\frac{\sigma_{t}(\nabla p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{i}^{(1)}({\bf x},t),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm in)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(1)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right)\right|+4m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta\sigma_{t}\\
&\leq D_{2}m^{-\frac{\beta}{d}}(\log m)^{2D+2\beta}+4m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta,\quad i\in[D]\end{split}

(92)

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}-\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-3/2}
and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}
.

B.5.2
Boundary of Near-Support

Let
\delta^{(2)}=m^{-(3DD_{1}^{2}+2\beta/d)}
and

\displaystyle\tau_{\rm bd}^{(2)}=3/2,\quad\widetilde{\tau}_{\rm bd}^{(2)}=1,

\displaystyle\tau_{\rm x}^{(2)}=\left\{\left(3DD_{1}^{2}+\frac{2\beta}{d}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\vee 1\right\}\left[D_{1}\vee\tau_{\rm min}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left\{(4D)^{\frac{3}{2}}+3\right\}\right],

\displaystyle\tau_{\rm t}^{(2)}=\left\{3D\left(3DD_{1}^{2}+\frac{2\beta}{d}\right)\right\}^{-1}\wedge\frac{1}{2}.

Let

For large enough

\displaystyle\delta^{(2)}\leq\widetilde{C}_{8},\quad 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}\leq\left\{\delta^{(2)}\right\}^{\tau_{\rm t}^{(2)}},\quad\left\{\log\left(1/\delta^{(2)}\right)\right\}^{-\widetilde{\tau}_{\rm bd}^{(2)}}\leq\tau_{2},\quad 2C_{S,1}m^{DD_{1}^{2}}\leq\left\{\delta^{(2)}\right\}^{-1}.

Also, a simple calculation yields that

\displaystyle\delta^{(2)}\leq m^{-\tau_{\rm min}},\quad\tau_{\rm x}^{(2)}\geq\tau_{\rm min}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left\{(4D)^{\frac{3}{2}}+3\right\},\quad D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}\leq\tau_{\rm x}^{(2)}\sqrt{\log\left(1/\delta^{(2)}\right)}.

Then, Proposition

implies that
that there exists a neural network

\displaystyle{\bf f}^{(2)}=(f_{1}^{(2)},\ldots,f_{D+1}^{(2)})^{\top}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L^{(2)},{\bf d}^{(2)},s^{(2)},M^{(2)})

\displaystyle s\leq\widetilde{C}_{6}\left\{\log\left(1/\delta^{(2)}\right)\right\}^{11+2D},\quad M\leq\exp\left(\widetilde{C}_{6}\left\{\log\left(1/\delta^{(2)}\right)\right\}^{2}\right),

such that

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{t}\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})\\
p_{t}({\bf x})\end{pmatrix}-{\bf f}^{(2)}({\bf x},t)\right\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{C}_{7}\delta^{(2)}\left\{\log\left(1/\delta^{(2)}\right)\right\}^{D}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}\epsilon_{2}

for

Lemma

implies that
C_{S,1}^{-1}m^{-DD_{1}^{2}}\leq p_{t}({\bf x})\leq C_{S,1}

Let

\displaystyle f_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm bd)}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm bd)},{\bf d}_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm bd)},s_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm bd)},M_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm bd)})

.

\displaystyle f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)},{\bf d}_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)},s_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)},M_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)})

\begin{split}&\left|\frac{\sigma_{t}(\nabla p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{i}^{(2)}({\bf x},t),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(2)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right)\right|\\
&\leq m^{-2\beta/d-1}+2(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})(4C_{S,1}^{2}+1)\epsilon_{2}m^{3DD_{1}^{2}}\leq D_{3}m^{-2\beta/d}(\log m)^{D},\quad i\in[D]\end{split}

(93)

for
\mu_{t}-\tau_{\rm min}^{3/2}\{(4D)^{3/2}+3\}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-3/2}\leq\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}+\sigma_{t}D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}
and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}
,
where
D_{3}=D_{3}(\beta,d,D,\widetilde{C}_{7},C_{S,1},C_{S,3},D_{1})
.
Consider functions
\widetilde{f}_{1}^{(2)},\ldots,\widetilde{f}_{D}^{(2)}:{\mathbb{R}}^{D}\times{\mathbb{R}}\to{\mathbb{R}}
such that

\displaystyle\widetilde{f}^{(2)}_{i}({\bf x},t)=f_{\rm mult}\left(f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{i}^{(2)}({\bf x},t),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(2)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right),f_{\rm rec}(f_{\sigma}(t))\right),\quad i\in[D]

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t\in{\mathbb{R}}
.
For large enough
m
so that
m^{3DD_{1}^{2}}\geq(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})^{2}m^{2DD_{1}^{2}}
, (
90
) and (
91
) implies that

\displaystyle\left|\sigma_{t}^{-1}f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{i}^{(2)}({\bf x},t),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(2)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right)-\widetilde{f}^{(2)}_{i}({\bf x},t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\delta+2m^{3DD_{1}^{2}}(1+m^{2\tau_{\rm min}})\delta\leq 5m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta,\quad i\in[D]

for
{\bf x}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{D}
and
t\geq m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}
.
Combining (
93
) with the last display, we have

\begin{split}&\left|(\nabla\log p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}-\widetilde{f}_{i}^{(2)}({\bf x},t)\right|\sigma_{t}\\
&\leq\left|\frac{\sigma_{t}(\nabla p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{i}^{(2)}({\bf x},t),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(2)}({\bf x},t)\right)\right)\right|+5m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta\sigma_{t}\\
&\leq D_{3}m^{-2\beta/d}(\log m)^{D}+5m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta\leq D_{3}m^{-\beta/d}+5m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta,\quad i\in[D]\end{split}

(94)

for
\mu_{t}-\tau_{\rm min}^{3/2}\{(4D)^{3/2}+3\}\{\log(1/\sigma_{t})\}^{-3/2}\leq\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}+\sigma_{t}D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}
and
m^{-\tau_{\rm min}}\leq t\leq 3m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}
, where the last ienquality holds for large enough
m
so that
(\log m)^{D}\leq m^{\beta/d}
.

B.5.3
Large
t

Let
m^{(3)}=\sqrt{m},\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}=1/2
,

\displaystyle\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)}=\left\lfloor\left\{\left(\frac{2D}{\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}}\right)\left(1+D\right)\left(\frac{2\beta}{d}+\frac{1}{2D}+3DD_{1}^{2}\right)+D\left(\frac{D+1-\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}}{\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}}\right)\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.\vee\left\{D(1+D)\left(\frac{\{\tau_{\rm max}\overline{\tau}\}\vee\{1/2D\}}{\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}}\right)-\frac{D+1-\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}}{\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}}\right\}\right\rfloor+1

\displaystyle\tau_{\rm x}^{(3)}=D_{1}\left\{\frac{2D\left(1+D\right)}{\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)}+D+1-\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Also, let

\displaystyle t_{*}=\left\{m^{(3)}\right\}^{-\frac{2-2\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}}{D}},\quad\delta^{(3)}=\left\{m^{(3)}\right\}^{-\frac{\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)}+D+1-\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}}{D(1+D)}},

and
\widetilde{C}_{9},\widetilde{C}_{10},\widetilde{C}_{11}
be the constants in Proposition
25
depending on
(D,K,\overline{\tau},\underline{\tau},\tau_{\rm x}^{(3)},\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)},\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)})
, where
(\tau_{\rm x},\tau_{\rm sm},\tau_{\rm low})
is replaced by
(\tau_{\rm x}^{(3)},\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)},\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)})
.
A simple calculation yields that

\displaystyle t_{*}=m^{-\frac{1}{2D}},\quad\left\{m^{(3)}\right\}^{-\frac{\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)}\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)}-(D+1-\tau_{\rm low}^{(3)})D}{D(1+D)}}\leq m^{-\frac{2\beta}{d}-\frac{1}{2D}-3DD_{1}^{2}},

\displaystyle\tau_{\rm x}^{(3)}\sqrt{\log\left(1/\delta^{(3)}\right)}=D_{1}\sqrt{\log m},\quad\delta^{(3)}\leq m^{-\frac{1}{2D}},\quad\tau_{\rm max}\log m\leq\overline{\tau}^{-1}\log\left(1/\delta^{(3)}\right).

Also, for large enough
m
, we have

\displaystyle m^{(3)}\geq\widetilde{C}_{11}\quad\text{and}\quad\left\{\log m^{(3)}\right\}^{\frac{D\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)}}{2}+D}\leq m^{\frac{1}{4D}}.

Then, Proposition
25
implies that
that there exists a neural network

\displaystyle{\bf f}^{(3)}=(f_{1}^{(3)},\ldots,f_{D+1}^{(3)})^{\top}\in\mathcal{F}_{\rm NN}(L^{(3)},{\bf d}^{(3)},s^{(3)},M^{(3)})

with

\displaystyle L^{(3)}\leq\widetilde{C}_{9}(\log m/2)^{4},\quad\|{\bf d}^{(3)}\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{C}_{9}\sqrt{m}(\log m/2)^{9},

\displaystyle s^{(3)}\leq\widetilde{C}_{9}\sqrt{m}(\log m/2)^{9},\quad M^{(3)}\leq\exp(\widetilde{C}_{9}\{\log m/2\}^{2}),

such that

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{t-t_{*}}\nabla p_{t}({\bf x})\\
p_{t}({\bf x})\end{pmatrix}-{\bf f}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*})\right\|_{\infty}\leq\widetilde{C}_{10}2^{-D(\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)}/2+1)}m^{-\frac{2\beta}{d}-\frac{1}{4D}-3DD_{1}^{2}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}\epsilon_{3}

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}+\sigma_{t}D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}
and
2m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm max}\log m
, where
t_{*}=m^{-1/(2D)}
.
Note that
C_{S,1}^{-1}m^{-DD_{1}^{2}}/2\leq p_{t}({\bf x})-\epsilon_{3}\leq f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t)\leq p_{t}({\bf x})+\epsilon_{3}\leq 2C_{S,1}
for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}+\sigma_{t}D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}
and
2m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm max}\log m
with large enough
m
so that
\epsilon_{3}\leq C_{S,1}^{-1}m^{-DD_{1}^{2}}/2
.
Then,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-f_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-\frac{1}{f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*})}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*})}-f_{{\rm rec}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\{p_{t}({\bf x})\wedge f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*})\}^{-2}\epsilon_{3}+\delta^{(2)}\leq 4C_{S,1}^{2}\epsilon_{3}m^{2DD_{1}^{2}}+\delta^{(2)}

\displaystyle\leq(4C_{S,1}^{2}+\widetilde{C}_{10}2^{-D(\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)}/2+1)})\epsilon_{3}m^{2DD_{1}^{2}}

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}+\sigma_{t}D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}
and
2m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm max}\log m
.
The last inequality holds because
2DD_{1}^{2}\geq 4D\tau_{\rm min}\geq 4/3\geq 1/(4D)
.
Then,

\begin{split}&\left|\frac{\sigma_{t-t_{*}}(\nabla p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{i}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*}),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*})\right)\right)\right|\\
&\leq m^{-2\beta/d-1}+2(C_{S,1}\vee C_{S,3})(4C_{S,1}^{2}+\widetilde{C}_{10}2^{-D(\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)}/2+1)})\epsilon_{3}m^{3DD_{1}^{2}}\leq D_{4}m^{-\beta/d-1/(4D)}\end{split}

(95)

for
\|{\bf x}\|_{\infty}\leq\mu_{t}+\sigma_{t}D_{1}\sqrt{\log m}
and
2m^{-\frac{1}{2D}}\leq t\leq\tau_{\rm max}\log m
.
where
D_{4}=D_{4}(D,\tau_{\rm sm}^{(3)},\widetilde{C}_{10},C_{S,1},C_{S,3})
.
Consider functions
\widetilde{f}_{1}^{(3)},\ldots,\widetilde{f}_{D}^{(3)}:{\mathbb{R}}^{D}\times{\mathbb{R}}\to{\mathbb{R}}
such that

\displaystyle\widetilde{f}^{(3)}_{i}({\bf x},t)=f_{\rm mult}\left(f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{i}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*}),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*})\right)\right),f_{\rm rec}(f_{\sigma}(t-t_{*}))\right),\quad i\in[D]

for

and

.
Combining with (

) and (

), we have

\displaystyle\left|\sigma_{t-t_{*}}^{-1}f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{i}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*}),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*}),\right)\right)-\widetilde{f}^{(3)}_{i}({\bf x},t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\delta+2m^{3DD_{1}^{2}}(1+m^{2\tau_{\rm min}})\delta\leq 5m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta,\quad i\in[D]

and

) with the last display, we have

\displaystyle\left|(\nabla\log p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}-\widetilde{f}_{i}^{(3)}({\bf x},t)\right|\sigma_{t-t_{*}}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{\sigma_{t-t_{*}}(\nabla p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}}{p_{t}({\bf x})}-f_{{\rm mult}}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{i}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*}),f_{\rm rec}^{(\rm bd)}\left(f_{D+1}^{(3)}({\bf x},t-t_{*})\right)\right)\right|+5m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta\sigma_{t-t_{*}}

\displaystyle\leq D_{4}m^{-\beta/d-1/(4D)}+5m^{5DD_{1}^{2}}\delta,\quad i\in[D]

and

\displaystyle\left|(\nabla\log p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}-\widetilde{f}_{i}^{(3)}({\bf x},t)\right|\sigma_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\left|(\nabla\log p_{t}({\bf x}))_{i}-\widetilde{f}_{i}^{(3)}({\bf x},t)\right|\sigma_{t-t_{*}}(2\underline{\tau})^{-1/2}m^{1/(4D)}

\displaystyle\leq D_{4}(2\underline{\tau})^{-1/2}\sqrt{\underline{\tau}}m^{-\beta/d}+5(2\underline{\tau})^{-1/2}\sqrt{\underline{\tau}}m^{5DD_{1}^{2}+1/(4D)}\delta,\quad i\in[D]

for

and

.

\delta=m^{-5DD_{1}^{2}-1/(4D)-\beta/d}
.
